HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_03b1a67863a1d7560c97","text":"4. **Life chosen becomes less than life.** Lexical evidence: حيي B013 life as benefit/saving good; موت/حيي double negation in 87:13; غثو B004 refuse in 87:5. Structural cue: the audience prefers الحياة after the wretched one cannot live. Assigned role: the noun حياة is exposed as ambiguous: biological nearness versus saving benefit. Abductive move: the packet supplies life-as-benefit and suspended non-life; I infer that preferring الدنيا chooses the weaker life-branch. Reading change: the ayah becomes ironic: choosing \"life\" can forfeit life.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0be4afdb8db9d7b77cb9","text":"87:17 immediately corrected the scale: the problem is not choosing life but choosing the near/lower against the better/remainer. The earlier pasture-to-chaff sequence made worldly life visibly perishable before it was named.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0f203c308629c6ebef82","text":"2. **Selfish exclusive possession.** Lexical evidence: أثر B006 taking exclusive possession for oneself; رعي B006 preserving a claim; خرج B003 due output/yield. Structural cue: the earlier pasture/provision and later better remainder frame this worldly preference. Assigned role: worldly life is treated as something to appropriate. Abductive move: the packet supplies exclusive preference and claim/yield; I infer that preferring الدنيا includes hoarding the provision that should lead to remembrance. Reading change: the ayah reads as possessive misdirection.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000011/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_13fc56973320415a4ebe","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:16:2\",\"root_000011\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Tercih, yakın hayatın yararını bütün iyilik kendisininmiş gibi tek başına sahiplenmeye kadar daralır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000383/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_23440b5cfb032e876e0f","text":"3. **Near life as unpurified pasture-life.** Lexical evidence: ح ي ي B002/B013 (life by rain/growth; saving good), ر ع ي B001 and غ ث و B002 in 87:4-5, ز ك و B001/B002 in 87:14. Structural cue: green pasture became black chaff before the explicit worldly-life critique. Assigned role: الحياة الدنيا is the life that grows but, without purification, dries into refuse. Abductive move: biological vitality is not the same as saving life. Reading change: the ayah does not reject life as such; it rejects the near life taken as final.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_544a909bf889282bd8ca","text":"1. **Preference for nearer life over purified success.** Lexical evidence: أثر B005 preferring/favoring; حيي B003 living being/life; دنو B002 nearer/lower/first side. Structural cue: \"بل\" interrupts after the successful purified/praying figure. Assigned role: تؤثرون names the wrong ordering; الدنيا names the near/lower object chosen. Abductive move: the packet supplies preference and near/lower life; I infer contrast with 87:17's afterlife. Reading change: the ayah diagnoses valuation, not mere desire.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000011/B005","root_000383/B013","root_000493/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_59bdf432d9aba8a8df43","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:16:2\",\"root_000011\",[\"B005\"]],[\"87:16:3\",\"root_000383\",[\"B013\"]],[\"87:16:4\",\"root_000493\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yakın hayatı seçmek, iyiliği yalnız yakınlığına göre değerlendiren ve kalıcılığı hesaba katmayan kusurlu bir tercih ölçüsüdür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000011/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5b77271fbe0a33fee2e8","text":"1. **The rebuke exposes proximity-driven selection.** `ء ث ر/B005` gives preferring one thing, `ح ي ي/B013` gives life as benefit or good, and `د ن و/B002` gives the nearer or first side. Immediate contrast with the later, better, and more enduring in 87:17 supplies the decision structure. Preference selects a benefit because it is near; the next ayah supplies durability as the omitted criterion. I infer temporal discounting from this near/later contrast. The ayah reads as a diagnosis of a defective selection rule, not merely identification of an object loved.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000011/B005","root_000383/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6ee908e104d0586fa7e7","text":"1. **Preference for the nearer/lower share.** Lexical evidence: ء ث ر B005/B006 (preferring; taking exclusive possession), ح ي ي B003 (animate life), د ن و B002/B003 (nearer/lower worldly side; baseness). Structural cue: بل interrupts the purified remembrance/prayer sequence. Assigned role: تؤثرون names the act of priority; الدنيا names the near/lower object chosen. Abductive move: moral failure is misordered preference rather than ignorance alone. Reading change: the ayah diagnoses desire's ordering.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000011/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7c2aa6a56dd23ecc947c","text":"3. **The root also reveals an abandoned trace.** `ء ث ر/B004` is following in a predecessor’s trace, and `ء ث ر/B003` is a remaining sign of what was. The prior sheets and named bearers in 87:18–19 make an older route materially available just after the preference statement. These dormant branches cast the verb against a path that could have been followed and a record that survives the choosers. I infer that the sheets constitute the trace. The ayah gains an irony: those who “prefer” the near do so while declining an enduring, already marked route.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9fc7714b9f73ed4f0b70","text":"3. **Following traces of the near world.** Lexical evidence: أثر B003 remaining sign/trace and B004 following someone's trace; دنو B001 nearness; ذكر B008 document and صحف B002 sheets in 87:18. Structural cue: the next ayah contrasts what is later/better/remaining, and the final ayat point to older records. Assigned role: أثر gives the chosen world a trace-path; the people follow the immediately visible mark instead of the older written sign. Abductive move: the packet supplies traces and sheets; I infer competing evidentiary trails. Reading change: \"prefer\" becomes \"track the nearest trace.\"","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000011/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_caf0e57625e3b2249a69","text":"87:17 immediately revealed that the fault lies in the criterion of preference: nearness has displaced goodness and persistence. More surprisingly, 87:18–19 activated the trace branches `ء ث ر/B003–B004`; a durable prior route remains in written sheets even while the addressed group declines to follow it.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ced909b539dc9161319e","text":"87:17 immediately corrected the scale of value: the later abode is خير and أبقى. 87:18-19 then made the correction authoritative by locating it in prior صحف, so the audience's preference is against an inherited record, not only against a new warning.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000383/B013"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d062ba7b11c2395e296a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:16:3\",\"root_000383\",[\"B013\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Hayatı seçtiğini sanan kişi, 87:13'teki yararsız süreklilik yüzünden kurtarıcı hayatı yitirme ironisiyle karşılaşır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000011/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d3e0a2e124f1bce3a815","text":"2. **Preference contracts into exclusive possession.** `ء ث ر/B006` is taking something exclusively for oneself. The second-person plural and the contrast with `خَيْرٌ وَأَبْقَىٰ` make the chosen near life function as a collectively repeated act of self-enclosure. The branch’s role is to sharpen preference into appropriation: immediate benefit is reserved as though it were the whole available good. I infer exclusivity from the root branch; the surface form directly states priority, not ownership. The reading changes from comparison to possessive narrowing.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000011/B003","root_000011/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d7acab676351a38b929b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:16:2\",\"root_000011\",[\"B003\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İlk sayfalarda kalan eski iz, yakın olanı seçenlerin önlerinde duran kalıcı yolu izlemeyi reddettiklerini gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000011/B003","root_000142/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e4beaad4ce5897a84c95","text":"2. **Following traces instead of enduring good.** Lexical evidence: ء ث ر B003/B004 (remaining trace; following someone's trace), ب ق ي B001/B002 in 87:17, ص ح ف B002 in 87:18. Structural cue: worldly life is set against the afterlife's remaining quality and former scriptures. Assigned role: آثار are the transient traces that lure the chooser. Abductive move: people may mistake visible residue/traces for lasting reality.
